New Delhi, the 3rd January 1972 
3 . 0 . 449. - It is hereby notified that in pursuance of 
clause ( d ) of sub - section ( 1 ) reud with sub - section ( 4 ) 
of section 4 of the Rajghat Samadhi Act, 1951 (41 of 
1951) , Shri H . K . L , Bhagat and Shri Madhu Dandavate , 
members of the House of the People , have been elected 
as members of the Rajghat Samadhi Committee in place 
of Shri Balraj Madhok and Dr. Sushila Nayar . 

2 . The Central Government hereby makes the fol 
lowing further amendments in the notification of the 
Government of India in the late Miistry of Works, 
Housing and Supply No . 19 / 2 /62 -WI, dated the 22nd 
August 1962, namely : 
In the said notification , for the entries 

" Shri Balraj Madhok and Dr. Sushila Nayar" , 
the following entries shall be gubstituted , namely : - - 

" Shri H . K . L . Bhagat 
Shri Madhu Dandavate "

( सम्पदा निवेशालय ) 
नयी दिल्ली , 14 जनवरी , 1972 , 
का० अ - 450 . -- राष्ट्रपति , मूल नियम 45 के उपबंधों 
के अनुसरण में , अनुपूरक नियम के भाग - 8, प्रभाग , 26- ख 
में अतिविष्ट , सरकारी निवासस्थानों का आवंटन (दिल्ली में 
साधारण पूल ) नियम , 1963 में और संशोधन करने के लिए 
रतद्वारा निम्नालिखत नियम बनाते है , अर्थात् : 
1. (1 ) इन नियों का नाम सरकारी निवास स्थानों का 

आवंटन ( दिल्ली में साधारण पूल ) पहला संशोधन 

नियम 1972 होगा । 
( 2 ) ये तुरन्स प्रवृत होंगे । 
2. सरकारी निवास स्थानों का आवंटन (दिल्ली में साधारण 
पूल ) नियम , 1963 में , नियम 317- ख. - 12 में , उप -नियम ( 1 ) 
में , "किसी ऐसे अधिकारी पर , जो स्वीकृति के बाद, आवंटन -पत्र 
की प्राप्ति की तारीख से पाठ दिन के भीतर उस जगह का कब्जा 
नहीं लेता " से प्रारम्भ होने वाले और “ इन में से जो भी पहने हो " 
के साथ समाप्त होने वाले वाक्य के मथान पर, निम्नलिखित वाक्य 
प्रतिस्थापित किया जायेगा , अर्थात् : - - 
" किसी ऐसे अधिकारी पर , जो स्वीकृति के बाद , आवंटन 

पत्र , की प्राप्ति की तारीख से आठ दिन के भीतर 
उस जगह का कलजा नहीं लेता, ऐसी तारीख से 
बारह दिन की अवधि तक लाइलम फीम प्रभारित 
को जायेगी , परन्तु इसमें अन्तर्विष्ट कोई भी 
बात उस दशा में लागू नहीं होगी जहां केन्द्रीय 
लोक निर्माण विभाग यह प्रमाणित कर दे कि वह 
जगह अधियोग के लिए अभी तैयार नही है पार 
उसके परिणामस्वरुप , अधिकारी पूर्वोक्त अवधि के 
भीतर उप जगह को अधिनोग में नहीं लेता है ।

New Delhi , the 14th January 1972 
8 . 0 . 450...-- In pursuance of the provisions of rule 
45 of the Fundamental Rules, the Presidro : hereby 
makes the following rules further to amend the Allot 
ment of Government Residences (General Pool in 
Delhi) Rules , 1763, contained in Part VII , Division 
XXVI- B of the Supplementary Rules, namely : 

1 . ( 1 ) These rules may be called thee Allotment of 
Government Residences (General Pool in Delhi) first 
Amendment Rules, 1972 . 

( 2 ) They shall come into force at onece. 

2. In the Allotment of Government Residences ( Ge 
neral Pool in Delhi ) Rules, 1963 , in rue 317 - B - 12, in 
gub - rule ( 1 ) , for the sentence beginning with " An on 
cer who , after acceptance, fails to take possession " 
and ending with " whichever is earlier ," the following 
sentence shall be substituted , namely : 
" An officer who , after acceptance , fails to take 

possession of that accommodation within 
eight days from the date of receipt of the 
allotment letter, shall be charged licence fee 
from such date upto a period of twelve days , 
provided nothing contained herein shall ap 
ply where the Central Public Works Depart 
ment certifies that the accommodation is not 
yet ready for occupation and as a result 
thereof the officer does not occupy the accom 
modation within the period aforesaid ."

एस० मो० 45 4. - - चलचित्र ( सेमर ) नियमावली 1958 
के नियम 10 के द्वारा प्रदत्त अधिकारो का प्रयोग करते हुए । केन्द्रीय 
सरकार : - - 
( 1 ) अध्यक्ष के सचिव श्री वी० एस० श्राफ की श्री अमर 

वर्मा, जिनको छुट्टी प्रदान की गई है, के स्थान पर 
स्थानापन्न अतिरिक्त प्रादेशिक अधिकारी , केन्द्रीय 
फिल्म सेंसर बोर्ड, बम्बई के रूप में नियुक्ति को 

27 दिसम्बर , 1971 से 16 जनवरी 1972 तक ; 
( 2 ) केन्द्रीय फिल्म सेंसर बोर्ड में अधीक्षक श्री आर० एस० 

सैगल की श्री वी० एस० श्राफ जिनकी पदोन्नति हुई 
है के स्थान पर , केन्द्रीय फिल्म सेंसर बोर्ड , बम्बई के 
अध्यक्ष के स्थानापन्न सचिव के रूप में नियुक्ति को 
27 दिसम्बर , 1971 से 16 जनवरी, 1972 तक , 
जारी रखते हैं ।

ient that coal is likely to be obtained from the lands

nga House , Ranchi or at the Omce of the Collector ,

Its Intention to prospect for coal therein .

of the said Act to the Revenue Oficer , National Coal 
Development Corporation Limited , Darbhanga House , 
Ranchi within 90 days from the date of publication in 
the Gazette of India of this notification .

का० प्रा० 459. - - यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार को ऐमा प्रतीत होता है कि इससे नपाबद्ध अनुसूची में वर्णित भूमि में से कोयला अधिप्राप्त 
होने की सम्भावना है । 

प्रतः अब, कोयला वाले क्षेत्र ( अर्जन और विकास ) अधिनियम , 1957 ( 1957 का 20 ) की धारा 4 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त 
क्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय मरकार एतद्द्वारा उसमें कोयले के लिए पूर्वेक्षण करने के अपने प्राशय की सूचना देती है । 

इस अधिसूचना के अतर्गत आने वाले क्षेत्र के रेखांक का निरीक्षण, राष्ट्रीय कोयला विकास निगम लिमिटेड ( राजस्व अनुभाग ), दर 
भंगा हाउस , रांची के कार्यालय अथवा कलक्टर, घेनकनाल (उड़ीसा ) के कार्यालय में अथवा कोयला नियंत्रक, 1, काउंसिल हाऊस स्ट्रीट , 
कल के कार्यालय में किया जा सकता है । 

इस अधिसूचना के अन्तर्गत आने वाली भूमि में हिनबद्ध सभी व्यक्ति , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 13 की उपधाग ( 7 ) में निदशित 
ममस्त मानचित्र, चार्ट और अन्य दस्तावेज, इस अधिसूचना के प्रकाशन की तारीख से 90 दिन के भ तर राजस्व अधिकारी, राष्ट्रीय कोयला 
विकास निगम लिमिटेड, दरभंगा हाऊम , गची को परिदत्त करेंगे ।

ing Centres all over the country .

का० प्रा० 461. -- भूमि अधिग्रहण अधिनियम , 
1894 ( 1894 का 1 ) की धारा 7 द्वारा प्रदत्त अधिकारों का 
प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा सूरत, लखनऊ, पटना , 
भुवनेश्वर, जलपाईगुड़ी, गौहाटी और दिल्ली में स्थित केन्द्रीय 
बाढ़ पूर्वसूचना प्रभागों ( केन्द्रीय जल तथा विद्युत आयोग ) के 
उपनिदेशकों को , समस्त देश में बाढ़ पूर्वसूचना केन्द्रों के लिए वेतार 


संयंत्रों के प्रतिष्ठापन के सम्बन्ध में अपेक्षित भूमि के अधिग्रहण 
के लिए मादेश लेने के लिए कलेक्टर को मादेश देने के लिए प्राधिकृत 
करती है । 

पावश 
भावेश दिया जाता है कि इस प्रधिपूचना की एक प्रति 
भारत के राज में प्रकाशित की जाए और इसे कृषि, वित्त 
( राजस्व विभाग ) गह पीर मंचार मंत्रालयों को भेज दी जाए । 

यह भी आदेश दिया जाता है कि यह अधिसूचना सभी 
र ज्य सरकारों को भेज दी जाए और उनमे यह प्रार्थना की जाय 
कि आम जानकारी के लिए इसे राज्य के राजपनों में प्रकाशित 
करवाद ।

क्षेत्रों में , जिसमें उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 4 और 5 के उपबंध 


[ No. F. S-38017(13)/ 7r- HI] 
प्रवृत्त नहीं हैं , अवस्थिति को ध्यान में रखते हुए उक्त कारखाने को 
उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 5-क के अधीन उद्ग्रहणीय नियोजक के 
विशेष अभिवाय के संदाय से , 14 मार्च, 1972 से 13 मार्च, 1973 
तक जिसमें वह दिन भी सम्मिलित है एक वर्ष की अवधि के लिए 
या तब तक के लिए जब तक कि उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 5 के 
उपबंध उन क्षेत्रों में प्रवृत्त नहीं हो जाते , जो भी पहने हो , एतद्द्वारा 
छूट देती है ।

का० प्रा० 489.-- - कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा अधिनियम , 
1948 ( 1948 का 34 ) की धारा 73च द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का 
प्रयोग करते हुए भारत सरकार के भूतपूर्व श्रम , रोजगार और 
पुनर्वास मन्त्रालय ( श्रम और रोजगार विभाग ) की अधिसूचना 
संख्या का० प्रा० 3050 तारीख 31 जुलाई, 1971 के क्रम में 
केन्द्रीय सरकार इससे उपाबद्ध अनमूची के स्तम्भ 4 में विनिर्दिष्ट 
कारखानों की उक्त अनुसूची के स्तम्भ 3 में विनिर्दिष्ट मैसूर राज्य 
के ऐसे क्षेत्रों में ,जिसमें उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 4 और 5 के 
उपबन्ध प्रवृत्त नहीं हैं , अवस्थिति को ध्यान में रखते हुए , उक्त 
कारखाने का उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 5क के अधीन उद्ग्रहणीय 
नियोजक के विशेष अभिदाय के संदाय से 13 दिसम्बर , 1971 
से 12 दिसम्बर , 1972 जिसमें वह दिन भी शामिल है तक 
एक वर्ष की और अवधि के लिए या तब तक के लिए जब तक कि 
उक्त अधिनियम के अध्याय 5 के उपबन्ध उन क्षेत्रों में प्रवृत्त नहीं 
हो जाते , जो भी पहले हो , एतद्द्वारा छूट देती है ।

$ . 0 . 513 . — Whereas by the notification of the

ment) No. S . O . 2710 , dated the 28th June, 1971, the

1947) the Central Government hereby declares the

कालावधि का छ : मास की और कालावधि के लिए बढ़ाया जाना 
अपेक्षित है ;

S . O . 517 . - Whereas the Central Government, having 
been satisfied that the public interest so required had , 
in purslaice of the provisions of sub - clause (vi) of 
clause (n ) of section 2 of the Industrial Disputes Act, 
1917 ( 14 of 1947 ) declared by the notification of the 
Government of India in the Ministry of Labour and 
Rehabilitation (Departinent of Labour and Employment) 
No. S . O . 3411, dated the 4th August , 1971 , service in the 
pyrites mining industry , to be a public utility service 
for the purposes of the said Act, for a period of six 
months from the 22nd August, 1971 ; 

And whereas the Central Government is of opinion 
that public interest requires extension of the said period 
by a further period of six months; 

Now , therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred 
by the proviso to sub- clause ( vi) of clause ( n ) of section 
2 of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 ( 14 of 1947), the 
Central Government hereby declares the said service to 
be a public utility service for the purposes of the said 
Act for a further period of six months from the 22nd 
February , 1972 .

एस० प्रो0 519 :-- - यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि भारत के खाद्य निगम के भंडार - अभिकर्तानों , इससे उपाबद्ध 
अनुसूची के स्तम्भ 1 में उल्लिखित और उक्त अनुसूची के स्तम्भ 2 में की तत्स्थानी प्रविष्टियों में उल्लिखित कर्मकारों के बीच , उसके 
स्तम्भ 3 में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है : - -- 


और यतः, केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिये निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है , 


अत , अब , प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम, 1947 ( 1947 का 14) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा प्रदत्त 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हये, केन्द्रीय सरकार तदद्वारा उक्त विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7 - क के अधीन गठित प्रौद्योगिक अधि . 
करण सं० 2, धनबाद, को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिये निर्देशित करती है ।

New Delhi , the 17th December 1971 
S . O . 520 . - -Whereas a vacancy has occurred in the 
office of the presiding officer of the Labour Court with 
headquarters at Bangalore , constituted by the notifica 
tion of the Government of India in the Ministry of 
Labour and Employment , No. S. O . 459, dated the 5th 
February , 19633; 

Now , therefore , in pursuance of the provisions of 
section 8 of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 (14 of 
1947) , the Central Government hereby appoints Shri 
P . Srinivasacharya as the presiding officer of the Labour 
Court constituted as aforesaid . 

___ [ No. F . S. 11025 / 46 / 71-LR.I. ] 
नई दिल्ली , 17 दिसम्बर 1971 
का० प्रा० 520 --- यत : भारत सरकार के श्रम और 
रोजगार मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना सं० का० प्रा० 459, तारीख 
5 फरवरी , 1963 द्वारा गठित श्रम न्यायालय , जिसका मुख्यालय , 
बंगलौर है , के पीठासीन अधिकारी का - रिक्त हो गया है , 

अतः अब, प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 8 के उपबंधों के अनुसरण मे , केन्द्रीय सरकार 
एतदद्वारा श्री पी० सिरीनिवासाचार्य को उपयुक्त रूप में 
गठित श्रम न्यायालय का पीठासीन अधिकारी नियुक्त करती है । 

[ सं० फा० एस० 11025/ 46/ 71 - एल० पार० 1 ] 


[सं० एल० - 42012 /18/ 71 - एल०पार III ] 

नई दिल्ली, 20 दिसम्बर , 1971 
का० श्रा० 521 - यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि 
इससे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिदि विषयों के बारे में सेंट्रल 
बैक आफ इंडिया के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके 
कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 

और यत . केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन 
के लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 

अतः, अब, औद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा

का० प्रा० 523 --- मुम्बई डार कर्मकार (नियोजन का विनियमन ) 
स्कीम 1956 में और आगे संशोधन करने के लिये एक स्कीम का 
निम्नलिखित प्रारूप जिसे केन्द्रीय सरकार डाक कर्मकार ( नियोजन 
का विनियमन ) अधिनियम , 1948 ( 1948 का 9 ) की धारा 4 
की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हए बनाने 
की प्रस्थापना करती है उम के द्वारा संभाव्यत : प्रभावित होने 
वाले सभी व्यक्तियो की जानकारी के लिये उक्त धारा द्वारा यथा 
अपेक्षित प्रकाशित किया जाता है , और एतद्द्वारा सूचना दी जाती 
है कि उतत प्रारूप पर 17 फरवरी , 1972 को या उस के पश्चात 
विवार किया जाएगा । 


इस प्रारूप के बारे में किमी भी व्यक्ति से यथाविनिर्दिष्ट 
नारीख के पूर्व प्राप्त हुए आक्षेपों या सुझावों पर केन्द्रीय सरकार 
धारा विचार किया जाएगा । 


स्कीम का प्ररूप 


1. इस स्कीम का नाम मुम्बई डाक कर्मकार (नियोजन का 
विनियमन ) संशोधन स्कीम , 1971 होगा ।

( 3 ) बोड़ अजित की गई मजदूरी का सम्बन्ध कर्मकारों 

के वास्तविक काम से विनियमो द्वारा स्थापित

अनुपात मे , बढ़ाई या घटाई जाएगी ;

कम नहीं होगा ।

Supreme Court did not think that Section 23C recog 
nises the inherent right of the employer to delare lay 
off for reasons which he may regard as sufficient or 
satisfactory in that behalf. As contended by the Union , 
the physical conditions of the Project which are re 
ported to be at variance from those estimated as per 
the contract cannot be brought under the category of 
" any other reason ." If the physical conditions were 
not what they were stated to be at the time of the 
contract it was open for the contractor to take re 
course to the terms of contract rewarding its revoration 
or revision . The employer is also at liberty to have 
closed down his egtablishment if he had chosen to do 
sa . Further, the provisions of the contract hetween 
Mormugao Port Trust and M / s . Ivan li utinovi - Pim 
borovide for a procedure including that of arbitration for 
settlement of differences and disagreements. The con 
tention of the manavenent that thcy could lay -off 
their workmen under their Standing Orders without 
wages cannot hold good as any provisions of the 
Standing Orders which are in contravention of any 
statutc cannot be enforced . 

7 . I have given careful consideration to the facts of 
the case and the various contentions of the parties and 
I have come to the corclusion that the lay -off resorted 
to by M / s , Ivan Milutinovic -Pim at Mormugan Harbour 
from 1 - 9 - 71 to 2 1 - 9 - 71 does not come strictly within 
the refirition nt " lay - off " as contained in Section 
? (kk ) of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947. As such , 
the lay - off cannot be said to he legal and in action 
which is not legal cannot he justified . I therefore an 
swer nart ( i ) of the referenre in the novative and de 
rid , that the workmen involved are entitled to receive 
full wagns for the lay - off nerio in question As the 
emolovers have already naid full woges for the lay - off 
period , though as an ad hoc arrangement under a 
memorandum of settlement, the same shall be treated 
as full and final payment to the concerned wolkmen , 
I give my award accordingly. 

O . MAHIPATHI.

AWARD 


The present reference arises out of order No. L - 2012 / 
86 / 71- LR . II dated New Delhi, the 30th July , 1971 passed 
hy the Central Government in respect of an industrial 
dispute betwien the parties mentioned above The 
subject matter of the dispute has been specified in the 
schedule to the said order which runs as follows: 
“Whether the stoppage of work of Shri Shakti 

Prasad Das , Attendance Clerk , with effect from 
the 18th September , 1970 , by the management 
of North Bhagatdih Colliery , Post Office Dhan 
sar, District Dhanbad , is justified ? If not, to 
what relief is the workman concerned enti 

tled ? " 
2 . Written statement on behalf of the employers was 
recsived on 13th September, 1971 and written statement 
on behalf of the workmen was received on 8th October, 
1971 The case was fixed for final hearing i.e . to say 
for evidenre and argument on the 1st of December , 
1971 at 2 30 P . M . On 1st December, 1971 Shri P . K . 
Bos ? . Advocate appeared on behalf of the employers 
and filed 10 items of documents ard Shri Rajballabh 
Praszd appeared on behalf of the workmen . With the 
consent of both parties the final hearing of the case 
was adjourned to 20th January , 1972 at 11 A . M . 

3 . When the case was taken up for hearing on 20th 
January , 1972 no one appeared on behalf of the work 
men nor was the workman concerned personally pre 
sent The employers however appeared through Shri 
S . S . Mukherjee, Advocate with Shri P . K Bose, 
Advocate . I waited for the appearance of the repre 
sentative of the workmen till after 12 .00 Noon . 

4 . From the conduct of the workmen it is clear that 
the workmen really have no dispute with the manage 
ment. Had there been a real dispute they would have 
taken proper care to represent their case before the 
Tribunal on the final date of hearing . I am therefore 
constrained to hold that in fact there is no dispute bet 
ween the management and the workmen . I according 
ly pass a no dispute award in this case. Let a copy

AWARD 
The present reference ai ises out of Order No. 2012 ) 
42 / 71 -LR . IT dated New Delhi, the 17th May, 1971 pass 
ed by the Central Government in respect of an indus 
tr al dispute between the parties mentioned above. The 
subject matter of the dispute has been specified in the 
Erhedule to the said order which runs as follows : 
“Whether the dismissal of Shri Bhola Gope, Night 

Guard , with effect from the 11th December, 
1970 by the management of Busserya Colliery 
of Messrs Busserya Colliery Company Private 
Limitfd , Post Office Kasunda , District Dhan 
bad is justified ? Tf not , to what relief is the 

workman entitled ? " 
2 . Written statement on behalf of the employers was 
received in the office of this Tribunal on 2nd June , 
1971 , and the written statement on behalf of the work 
man was received on 5th June , 1971 . 32 items of docu 
ments were filed on behalf of the employers on 23rd 
July , 1971 and 18 more documents were filed by the 
emplɔyers on 9th August, 1971. No document has been 
filed by the workman . 

3 . The case was fixed for hearing on 15th October , 
1971 on a preliminary point, namely , whether the 
dapartmental enquiry was properly conducted or not. 
The preliminary point was heard on that date . Two 
witnesses were examined on behalf of the employers 
No witness was examined on behalf of the workmen

(Part II 


4 . On 28th October, 1971 Shri B . Lal appearing on 
behalf of the workman Aled an application stating that 
as the management of the colliery concern had been 
taken over by the Central Government, the Custodian 
General and the Custodian should be impleaded as par 
ties to the proceedings. Notices were issued to the Cus 
todian General and the Custodian to show cause by 18th 
November 1971 , why they should not be added as parties 
to the proceeding . Two written statements in identical 
language were fled on 10th January , 1972 by Shri J . N . P . 
Şahi, one on behalf of the Custodian General and another 
on behall of the Custodian . It may be noted that the 
hearing on the preliminary point took place on 15th 
October , 1971 in the absence of the Custodian General 
and the Custodian because the application for adding 
them parties was Aled subsequent to that date . 13th 
January , 1972 was fixed for passing an order on the 
preliminary point. 

5 . I propose to consider the evidence and arguments 
on the preliminary point in order to give my decision 
on the preliminary point. Siri Bhola Gope the wook 
man concerned was appointed on 1st December , 1965 
as a Night Guard or Depot Guard in the Busserya 
Colliery . A chargeshçet dated 17th July , 1970 was 
Issued to Shri Bhola Gope for riotous and disorderly 
behaviour, for disobedience of lawlul order and also 
for remaining absent from duty without leave. Bhola 
Gope submitted his reply dated 26th August, 1971 to 
the above chargesheet , and the said reply was received 
by the employers on 3rd September , 1971). 

6 . Another chargesheet dated 2012 August , 1970 was 
given to him on the allegation that he indulged in 
further riotous and disorderly behaviour on 17th 
August , 1970 Shri Bhola Gope submitted his reply dated 
26th August 1970 to the above chargcsheet and the said 
was received by the management on 3rd September, 
1970 . 

7 . Two separate domestic enquiries were held into the 
two chargesheets respectively dated 17th July , 1970 and 
20th August , 1970. It has been alleged in paragraph 
11 of the written statement submitied by the employers 
that in spite of repeated chances given to Shri Bhola 
Gope, he refused to attend the domestic enquiries and 
as such they had to be held in his absence . 

8 . In paragraph 12 of the said written stat :ment it 
has been stated that in the departmental enquiries 
misconducts mentioned in the chargeshects dated 17th 
July , 1970 and 20th August, 1970 were satisfactorily 
established and that Shri Bhola Gope was therefore 
dismissed by a letter dated 11th December , 1970 by che 
Director of the company atter he was satisfied that the 
misconducts against Shri Bhola Gope had been satis 
factorily established . It has also boen stated in para 
graph 13 that the past records of Sh . i bhola Gope were 
also not satisfactory and that he was suspended or 
warned several times in the past for proved miscon 
ducts . 

9 . The workmen have made serious allegations again 1 
the management in their written statement dated 2nd 
June, 1971. They have stated in paragraphs 3 and 4 that 
in 1969 workmen of this colliery enrolled themselves 
as members of the Koyala Mazdoor Panchayat, that they 
started some agitation for their genuine grievances . 
that Bhola Gope took a leading part in this agitationi 
which culminated in a criminal procesling U / S 107 
Cr. P . C . avainst some of his co -workors . It has further 
been stated that while Ehola Gope was working in 
the once he was transferred on 15th February , 1970 to 
work as a Night Guard at the back side of the Director s 
Bungalow and that he accepted th : said order and 
started his work at that place . 

10 . Their further case is that as the agilation con 
tinued for payment of wages as per recommendations 
of the Central Wage Board for Coal Mining Inslustry , 
the Manager changed Bhola Gope s place of duty from 
Director s Bungalow to No . 3 siding in a distant isolated 
area . 


11. It has been alleged in paragraph 8 of the written 
statement that Bhola Gope tell ill with effect from 10th 
July , 1970 and was under the treatment of Dr. M . P . 
Singh , Civil Assistant Surgeon , Sadar Hospital at 
Dhanbad and that he sent an application with medical 
certifcate to the Manager of the Colliery requesting 
for grant of sick leave . It has further been alleged in 
paragraph 9 that on receipt of the transfer order on 
16th July , 1970 Bhola Gope went to the office of the 
Manager to hand over the medical certificate and appli 
quation again with a prayer to grant him sick leave and 
that at the interview he also requested that his duty 
place should be changed from No. 3 siding to any other 
suitable place. 

12 . Their further case is that this simple request ways 
concocted as a threat to the Personnel Officer , that on 
the same day namely 10th July , 1970 a report was made 
to the Police alleging apprehension of breach of peace 
and that Bhola Gope s co -workers were implicated in a 
criminal proceeding U / S 107 Cr. P . C . 

13 . According to the workmen , Bhola Gope remained 
sick under the treatment of Dr. M . P . Singh , Civil 
Assistant Surgeon , Sadar Hospital, Dhanbad till 9th 
August, 1970 when he was declared At to join his duties 
but he was not allowed to join his duty on 10th August , 
1970 after his recovery from tllness . They have further 
stated that by a letter dated 12th August, 1970 received 
by Bhola Gope on 14th August , 1970 he was asked to 
give his reply to the chargesheet dated 17th July , 1970 . 

14 . They admit that by a letter dated 20th August. 
1970 another allegation for riotous behaviour and use 
of filthy language was made against Bhola Gope by 
the Manager . They have also given the gist of the 
allegation in paragraph 14 of_ their written statement. 

15 . On the question of departmental enquiry the ver 
sion of the workmen is as follows. A date of enquiry 
Into the allegations was fixed for 21st October , 1970 . 
Bhola Gope went to the office of the Manager, the place 
appointed for the enquiry but there was none to hold 
the enquiry and he reported to the Manager in writing 
and also forwarded a copy of the same to the Assistant 
Labou , Commissioner ( C ) , Dhanbad for information . 
He received two letters both datel 26th October, 1970 
on 7th November , 1970 by registered post in which 
was stated that enquiries would be held in the two 
chargesheets on 4th November, 1970 and 7th November, 
1970 . 


16 . They admit that the next dates for the two 
enquiries were Axed on 8th December, 1970 and 9th 
December, 1970. Their complaint is that the name of 
the Enquiry Oncer was never Intimated to Bhola Gope. 
Their further complaint is that though Bhola Gope 
went to the place of enquiry , there was none to hold 
th ? enquiry and that he reported the same to the 
Manager before leaving the place of enquiry . They 
have characterised the ex - parte enquiries that took 
place as merely a show . Their grievance is that Bhola 
Gone was denied the opportunity of listening to the wit 
nesses on behalf of the prosecution and put his defence 
witnesses . 

17 . They have stated in paragraph 21 that as the 
copies of statements recorded on behalf of tho prosecu 
tion were neither handed over nor sent to Bhola Gope 
Tuy registered post, he could not produce his witnesses 
before the Enquiry Officer . They have also complained 
that no date was fixed for the production of defence 
witnesses to counter the statements of the prosecution 
witnesses. The enquiry proceedings , according to them , 
are vitiated , biased and perverse . They assert that as 
natural justice has been denied to Bhola Gone the pro 
cearlings should be quashed , 

18 . Let me now consider the evidence for the pur 
re of a scortaining whether the two enquiries were 
properly held or not and whether the management was 
justified in dismissing him on the two reports sub 
mitted by the Enquiry Officer in respect of the two

ived medical certificate in support of the alleged illness 
of Bhola Gope . He also pointed out that he had not 
received any leave application . He expressed his in 
ability to find any reason why Shri Bhola Gope should 
not be able to work at No. 3 siding when he was ready 
and fit to work at No. 2 and 4 sidings or 39 incline. 


The above copy of medical certificate has been addreg 
sed to the Manager, Basuria colliery on which it has 
also been written " To whom it may concern " . It does 
not bear any signature or Hospital seal. 


We have further received on 11th August, 1970 a 
similar copy of above medical certificate dated 8th 
August 1970 sent by you under registered post with 
A . D . without any application with it. 

There appears to be some interpolation in the date 
of both the copies of the medical certificates . 


Even after your transfer to work in No. 3 siding 
no complaint of your alleged illness was heard from 
you nor you reported for treatment before the colliery 
Medical Officer . 


You may join your duty at No. 3 siding and submit 
satisfactory explanation to the chargesheet dated 17th 
July , 1970 within 48 hours from the receipt of this 
letter . ( A copy of chargesheet is enclosed ) . 

Yours faithfully , 
Sd . /

in nature . He was threatened with action if he falled 
to join his duty at No. 3 şiding . I am therefore not

proceedings that took place on the basis of this charge

3 . Sri S , A . Aktar is present on behaf of the manage 
ment and Sri R . Sen is present for the workmen 
Heard both the representatives . Considered cach of 
the items of Clause ( i ) , sub - clauses ( a ) , ( b ) , ( c ) and 
( d ) of the compromise petition . Sub - clause ( a ) is 
clearly for the benefit of the workman . His pay would 
be increased by 37 paise per day with immediate effect 
1. e. 11th November 1971. The worker , by sub - clause 
(b ) , would get after 3 months froin thc date of the 
compromise , provided his work is found satisfactory , a 
lift to the post of Loading Supervisor and would be 
graded as a monthly paid staff with a starting basic 
pay of Rş, 180 per month . This clause is also very 
much beneficial to the workman concerncd . By sub 
clauses ( c ) and ( d ) the Union shall have no claim 
whatsoever in relation to the dispute and the dispute 
is fully and finally settled between the parties. 

4 . Considering each and every clauses of the settle 
ment and hearing the representatives of the parties , I 
find that the compromise is just , fair and equitable and 
resolves the dispute between the parties . According 
ly , I record the compromise and pass an award in terms 
of the compromise field before me to which neither 
party objects . Let the petition of compromise form 
part of this award .

इससे उपाबद्ध अनसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में सिंगनी

विद्यमान है ; 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
लिए निदशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 


का० प्रा० 533 . ----यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार की गय है कि

के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 

और यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ;

कि इससे उपाबद अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मैसर्स

नई दिल्ली, 25 अक्तूबर, 1971 
का० प्रा० 537.-- यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि 
इससे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मैसर्स खास 
गोविन्दपुर कोल कम्पनी प्राइवेट लिमिटेड की खास गोविन्दपुर 
कोलियरी, डाकघर कतरासगढ़, जिला धनबाद के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध 
नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद 
विद्यमान है । 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 


अतः, अब , प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हए कन्द्रीय सरकार एतदद्वारा 
उक्त विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7- क के अधीन गठित 
फन्द्रीय सरकार प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण (संख्या 1 ) , धनबाद को 
न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए निर्देशित करती है । 


और यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए 
निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 
। अतः, अब, प्रौद्योगिक विवाव अधिनियम, 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( ध ) द्वारा

उक्त विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7 - क के अधीन गठित 
केन्द्रीय सरकार प्रौद्योगिक मधिकरण ( संख्या 1 ) धनबाद को 
न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए निर्देशित करती है ।

Disputes Act , 1947 (14 of 1947 ), theCentralGovernment hereby constitutes anIndustrial Tribunal with Shri P. S . Ananth as the 
Presiding Officer with headquarters at Hyderabad , withdraws the proceedings in relation to the said disputes and the applica 
tions under the provison to sub - section ( 2 ) of section 33 and complaints under sectior 32A ( f the said Act and arising out the 
said disputes, applications and complaints from Shri T . Chandrasekhara Reddy and trerafcre the same to Shri P . S . Ananth , 
Presiding Officer , Industrial Tribunal , Hyderabad , for the disposal of the said disputes , applications and complaints with the 
direction that the said Tribunal shall proceed with the proceedings from the stage at which they are transferred to it and 
dispose of the same according to law .

पन पोरशिकायतें (जिन्हें इसके बाद उक्त विवाद प्रावेदन पत्र और शिकायतों के रूप में निर्दिष्ट किया गया है ) , श्री टी० चन्द्रशेखर रेही , 
पीठासीन अधिकारी, केन्द्रीय प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण, जिनका मुख्यालय हैदराबाद में है, के समक्ष लम्बित थें ; 

और यतः श्री टी० चन्द्रशेखर रेड्डी की सेवाएं . अब उपलब्ध नहीं रही हैं ;

अतः, अब, मोद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 1947 का 14 ) की धारा 7 - 2 और धारा 33 - ख की उपधारा ( i ) द्वार 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते ए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतदद्वारा एक प्रौद्योगिक अधिकरण गठित करती है जिसके प ठासीन अधिकारी श्री पी० एस०मानन्ध 
होंगे, जिनका मुख्यालय हैदराबाद होगा , श्री टी० चन्द्रशेखर रेड्डी से उक्त विदो और भावेदन पत्रों से सम्बन्ध कार्यवाहियों को उक्त अधिनियम 
की धारा 33 की उपधारा ( 2) के परन्तुक के अधीन और शिकायतों से सम्बन्ध कार्यवाहियों को धारा 33 के अधीन वापस लेती है और उन्हें श्री 
पी० एस० मनन्थ , पीठासीन अधिकारी , प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण , हैदराबाद को उक्त विवादों , पावेदन पत्रों और शिकायतों के निपटान के लिए 
निदेश के साथ स्थानान्तरित करती है कि उक्त अधिकरण मोर मागे कार्यवाहियां उसी प्रक्रम से करेगाजिस पर वे उसे स्थानान्तरित की जाएं 
और विधि के अनुसार उनका निपटार करेगा ।

10. एम० पी० सं० 24 / 71 


New Delhi, the 16th November 1971 
S . O . 541 . - Whereas the Central Government is of 
opinion that an industrial dispute exists between the 
employers in relation to the management of Lachipur 
Colliery of Messrs Katras Jharla Coal Company Limit 
ed , Post Office Kajoragram , District Burdwan and their 
workmen in respect of the matters specified in the 
Schedule hereto annexed ; 

And , whereas, the Central Government considers it 
desirable to refer the said dispute for adjudication ; 

Now , therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred 
by clause ( d ) of sub - section ( 1 ) of section 10 of the 
Industrial Disputes Act , 1947 ( 14 of 1947 ) , the Cen 
tral Government hereby refers the said dispute for 
adjudication to the Central Government Industrial 
Tribunal, Calcutta , constituted under section 7A of the 
sald Act . 

SCHEDULE 
"Whether the action of the management of Lachipur 
Colliery of Messrs Katras Jharia Coal Company Limit 
ed , Post Office Kajoragram, District. Burdwan in stop 
ping the work of ( 1 ) Sarvashri Azad Khan , (2 ) Dina 
nath Shaw , ( 3 ) Md. Nasiruddin, ( 4 ) Dahara Turi, ( 5 ) 
Suleman Mia, Stone Cutters and ( 6 ) Isrile Mia, ( 7 ) 
Maluddin Mla , ( 8 ) Ambika Singh , ( 9 ) Saidagar Yadab , 
( 10 ) Jaman Mla , ( 11) Fakir Chand, ( 12 ) Chandrika 
Yadab , ( 19) Mahabir Das, ( 14 ) Majo Bouri, ( 15 ) Moti 
Rajak, ( 16 ) Barhamdeo Modi, ( 17) Triloke Yadab, (18 ) 
Bhuneswar Yadav , Stone Cutter Mazdoor , with effect 
from the 4th August, 1970 is justified ? If not, what 
relief are the workmen entitled to ? " 

[ No. L / 1912 / 80 / 71 -LRII .] 


[ संख्या एल0 / 2125/ 3/ 71 -एल०मार० - 2] 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार एतदद्वारा उक्त 
विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7 - क के अधीन गठित केन्द्रीय 
सरकार औद्योगिक प्रधिकरण , कलकत्ता को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करती है । 

अनुसूची 
" क्या मैसर्स कतरस झरिया कोल कम्पनी लिमिड की 

लचिपुर कोलियरी, डाकघर कजोराग्राम, जिला बर्दवान 
की ( 1 ) सर्वश्री पाजाद खां , ( 2 ) दीननाथ शो ,

जिला धनबाद के प्रबध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों 
के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ;

लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ;

का०मा० 544. - यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे 
उपाबर अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में दी सेन्द्रा बांसजोरा 
कोलियरी कम्पनी प्राइवेट लिमिटेड की सेन्द्रा बांसजोरा कोलियरी , 
डाकपर सरिया जिला धनबाद के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और 
उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है । 

और यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है । 

अतः, अब, प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा प्रदत्त 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए , केन्द्रीय सरकारएतद्वारा उक्त विवाद 
को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7 क के अधीन गठित केन्द्रीय सरकार 
पोयोगिक प्रधिकरण ( संख्या 1 ) , धनबाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करती है । 


का० प्रा० 545.-- - यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे 
उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में अमलगामेटड

का०मा० 549.. . यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे 
उपाबपन सूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मैसर्स इक्वीटेबल 
कोल कम्पनी लिमिटेड, के दिशेरगढ़ कार्यालय डाकवर दिशेरगढ़ 
जिला बर्दवान के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों 
के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है । 


का० प्रा० 550 - - यतः नेशनल मिनरल्स डेवेलपमेंट कारपो 
रेशन लिमिटड के डोनीमलाई आयरन और प्रोजेक्ट डाकघर सान्दुर 
के प्रबन्ध के सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों जिनका प्रतिनि . 
धित्व डोनीमलाई पायरन और प्रोजेक्ट इम्प्लाईज ऐसोसिएशन सान्दर 
फरती है ने प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम 1947 ( 1947 का 14 ) 
की धारा 10 की उप-धारा ( 2 ) के अधीन संयुक्त रूप से केन्द्रीय 
सरकार को आवेदन किया है कि वह उनके बीच विद्यमान प्रौद्यो 
गिक विवाद को उक्त प्रावेदन में उपणित और इससे उपाबर 
अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में किसी प्रौद्योगिक अधि 
करण को निर्देशित करें , 


मोर यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्याय निर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करना यांछनीय समझती है ; 

प्रतः, अब , प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 का 
14) कीधारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा प्रदत्त 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए , केन्द्रीय सरकार एतदद्वारा उक्त 
विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7- क के अधीन गठित केन्द्रीय 
सरकार प्रायोगिक प्रधिकरण , कलकत्ता को न्याय निर्णयन के लिए 
निदेशित करती है । 


और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार का समाधान हो गया है कि 
आवेदन करने वाले व्यक्ति प्रत्येक पक्ष के बहुमत का प्रतिनिधित्व 
फरते हैं ; 


अनुसूची 
" क्या मैसर्स इक्वीटेबल कोल कम्पनी लिमिटेड , डाकघर 
दिशेरगढ़ , जिला बर्दवान के प्रबन्ध मण्डल की श्री दाहारी 
राउण , रक्षक दिशेरगढ़ कार्यालय को 31 जुलाई 
1971 से पदव्युत करने की कार्यवाही न्यायोचित 


अतः अब प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम 1947 ( 1947 
का 14) की धारा 7 - क भार धारा 10 का उपधारा 2द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुये केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा 
एक प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण गठित करती है जिसके पीठासीन अधि 
कारी श्री पी० एस० प्रानन्थ होंगे जिनका मुख्यालय हैदराबाद 
होगा और उक्त विवाद को उक्त प्रौद्योगिक अधिकरण को न्याय 
निर्णयन के लिये निर्देशित करती है ।

अनुसूची

डोनीभलाई आयरन ओर प्रोजक्ट , डाकघर सान्दुर के प्रवन्ध

गोपालकृष्णन् , वैल्डर बी पार जी० एस० पिल्ले , उच्च श्रेणी 
लिपिक को 6 मई , 1971 से घेवा से पदच्युत करने की कार्यवाही

इससे उपाबद्ध अनूसूची मे विनिदिष्ट विषयों के बारे में रियल 
काजोरा कोलियरी , डाकघर काजोराग्राम, जिला बर्दवान के प्रबन्ध 
से सम्बद नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक 
विवाद वियमान है ।

के लिए निर्देशित करना वाछनीय समझती है ;

का 14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा

उक्त विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7-क के मधीन गठित 
फेन्द्रीय सरकार मौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण , कलकत्ता को न्यायनिर्णयन 
के लिए निर्देशित करती है ।

का० प्रा० 553 . . .-- - यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि 
इमसें उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मैसर्स 
वैस्टर्न गाल कोल फील्डस लिमिटेड, बेतरी कापर प्रोजेक्ट , 
खेतरी नगर के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के 
बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है । 

और यतः केनीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए 
निर्देशित करमा वाछनीय समझती है ; 

अतः, अब , प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 7- क और धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड 
( घ ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हए, केन्द्रीय सरकार 
एतदद्वारा एक प्रौद्योगिक अधिकरण गठित करती है जिसके 
पीठासीन अधिकारी श्री गोपाल नारायण शर्मा होगे, जिनका मुरू 
यालय जयपुर होगा और उक्त विवाद को उक्त प्रायोगिक प्रधिकरण 
को न्यायनिर्णन के लिए निशित करती है । 

अनुसूची 
" झ्या हाताल की अवधि 7 सितम्बर, 1971 से 25 सितम्बर , 

1971 ( 4 बजे अपराहन ) तक समझी जानी चाहिए

क लिए श्रमिको को प्रबन्ध मण्डल द्वारा मजदूरी देय 
होनी चाहिए और अगर हां तो किस सीमा तक ? "

फा०मा०554.- यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे 
उपाबद अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मैसर्स टाटा 
आयरन एण स्टील कम्पनी लिमिटेड की मालकेरा चोयटुडिह 
कोलियारी, डाकघर मालकेरा , जिला धनबाद के प्रबन्ध से 
सम्बद नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक 
बिवाव विद्यमान है । 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है : 

अतः अब , प्रोबोगिक विवाद अधिनियम, 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा 
उक्त विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7 - क के अधीन गठित 
केन्द्रीय सरकार प्रायोगिक प्रधिकरण ( संख्या 1 ), धनबाद 
को पायनिर्णयन के लिए निर्देशित करती है । 

मनसपी

कोलियरी , डाकघर जीलगोरा, जिला धनबाद के प्रबन्ध से 
सम्बर नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक माधोगिक 
विवाद विधमान है । 


और यतः, केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन . 
के लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 

प्रतः अब, प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 
का 14) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( प ) द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतदद्वारा 
विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा क के अधीन गठित

का०मा० 557. यत: केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे 
उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिदिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मैम दी ओरिएंटल 

कोल कम्पनी लिमिटेड की पूतकी कोलियरी , डाकघर कुसुन्डा 
जिला धनबाद के प्रबन्ध से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों 
के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है । 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के 
लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है । 

प्रतः, अब, प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम, 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्द्वारा 
उक्त विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7 के अधीन गठित 
केन्द्रीय सरकार प्रीयागिक अधिकरण , धनबाद- 1 को न्यायनिर्णयन 
के लिए निर्देशित करती है । 

अनुसूची

कोलियारी , डाकघर कुसुन्डा ,जिला धनबाद के प्रबन्ध मण्डल को , 
श्री यूसुफ मियां, खनिक को 20 अक्तूबर, 1970 से पदच्युत करने 
की कार्यवाही न्यायोचित है ? यदि नहीं , तो कर्मकार सम्बन्धित 
किस मनतोष का हकदार है ? "

It is hereby agreed between the above parties that 
in partial modlication of the Memorandum of Settle 
ment dated 27th January , 1970 , made between the 
parties in respect of the Constitution of the Arbitrators , 
the following industrial disputes be referred to the sole 
arbitration of Shri Mohan Lal Sukhadia , former Chief 
Minister of Rajasthan , whose consent to arbitrate in 
the matters hereinafter mentioned has been obtained . 

I. SpeciAc matters in dispute in respect of the 
Limestone Quarries at Phallodi and Bajrakho / Bheron 
pura on which arbitration is required : 
1. Whether Bonus for the accounting year 1964 -65 

already paid by M / S . Jaipur Udyog Ltd ., 
Sawaimadhopur, @ 4 . 78 % as worked out under 
the Profil and Loss Accounts under Balance 
Şheet was in accordance with the Payment of 
Bonus Act, 1965 or not ? And whether work 
men , represented by he Cement Works Karam 
chari Sangh , Sawaimadhopur, are entitled to 

any relief for the year in question , under law ? 
2 . Whether bonus for the accounting years 1965 -66, 

1966 - 67 , 1967 -68 and 1968 -69. already paid by 
M / s . Jaipur Udyog Limited , Sawaimadhopur , 
@ 4 per cent, 4 per cent, 5 per cent and 4 per 
cent respectively , according to the allocable 
surplus worked out under the Profit and Loss 
Accounts under Balance Sheet is in accordance 
with the Payment of Bonus Act , 1965 or not ? 
And whether workmen represented by the 
Cement Works Karamchari Sangh , Sawalma 
dhopur, are entitled to any relief for the years 

in question , under law ? 
3 . Whether the workmen in Quarries , whose names 

are mentioned in Annexure I have not been 
suitably fixed according to the Second Wage 
Board for Cement Industry and if so , to what 

relief are they entitled ? 
4 . Whether the workinen whose names are given 

in Annexure II and who were dismissed from 
the services on the charge of assault , aro 

entitled to any relief ? If so , to what extent ? 
5 . Whether the demand to have the same age of 

superannuation of workmen in Phallodi Quar 
ries, as has been provided in the Standing 
Orders of the Cement Factory at Sawaimadho 

pur, is justified ? 
6 . Whether in view of the past Agreements , the 

demand of the Sangh for re-designation and 
regradation of workers of Quarries whose 
names are mentioned in Annexure III in accord 
ance with their jobs, is justifed ? If so , to 

what relief are the workmen entitled ? 
7. Whether Sick Leave with pay should be allowed 

to be accumulated upto 6 years instead of 3 
years as at present, in case of Operatives and 
5 years in the case of members of staff and 
whether half of the accumulated Sick -Leave 
with pay be permitted to be encashed or not, 

at the time of leaving of service ?, 
8 Whether any revision should be made in the 

existing rate of Stone -Cutters at the Quarries , 

if so , to what extent ? 
9 . Whether the existing scheme of Gratuity needs 

any revision , and if so , in what manner ? 
10 . Whether the existing provisions of Dust Allow 

ance , Equipment Allowance and Seasonal 
Uniforms be extended to new categories of 
employees as mentioned in Annexure IV keep 
ing in view the provisions of the Wage Board 

Report ? 
The Parties Agree that joint applications shall be 
flled before the Industrial Tribunal Rajasthan , Jaipur , 
with the request to pags " No Dispute Award " in the 
following cases :

Neur Delhi, the 17th December 1971 
S . O . 575. - Whereas the Central Government is of 
opinion that an industrlal dispute exists between the 
employers in relation to the management of Messrs 
J . M . Baxi and Company , Madras and thelr workmen in 
respect of the matters spccified in the Schedule hereto 
annexec! ; 

And , whereas, the Central Government considers it 
desirable to refer the said dispute for adjudication ; 

Now , therefore , in exercise of the powers conferred by 
Section 7A and clause ( d ) of sub - section ( 1 ) of section 
10 of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 (14 of 1947) , the 
Central Government hereby constitutes an Industrial 
Tribunal of which Shri K . Seetharama Rao shall be the 
Presiding Omcer , with headquarters at Madras and 
refers the said dispute for adjudication io the said Trı 
bunal . 


अध्यक्ष " 


ŞCHEDULE

Baxi and Company , Madras - 1 are entitled to a bonus 
of 8 - 1 / 3 per cent for the years 1969 - 70 and 1970 71 on

be tho rate of bonus payable to them ? "

MINISTRY OF COMMUNICATIONS 

( P. & T. Board ) 
New Delhi, the 4th February 1972 
S . O . 676 . - In exercise of the powers conferred 
by section 7 of the Indian Post Office Act , 1898 ( 6 of 
1898 ) , the Central Government hereby makes the 
following cules further to amend the Indian Post 
Office Rules, 1933, nemely : -- 

1. ( 1 ) These rules may be called the Indian Post 
Office ( 5th Amendment) Rules, 1972. 

( 2 ) They shall come into force on the 13th 
February, 1972 . 

2. In rule 107 of the Indian Post Office Rule 1933, 
to sub -rule ( 1 ) , the following proviso shall be added , 
namely : 
" Provided that a late fee of ten paise shall be 

charged on every registered article presented 
before selected post offices, kept open on 
all days in the week including Sundays, for 
booking during the hours between 4 - 30 P. M . 
and 8- 30 P. M . 

[ No. 34 - 4 / 71 - CI.] 

B . SRINIVASAN , 
Assistant Director General ( M ) .

का० प्रा० 579 भारत मरकार के वित्त मंत्रालय ( आर्थिक 
कार्य विभाग ) के पहली फरवरी 1969 के का० प्रा० संख्या 558 
की अधिसूचना के अन्तर्गत प्रकाशित पुजी निर्गम ( छट ) प्रादेश 
1969 के खण्ड 5 के उपखण्ड ( ix ) के अनुसरण में केन्द्रीय सरकार 
एतवहारा यह निदेश देती है कि 1972 के कैनण्डर वर्ष के दौरान

ELECTION COMMISSION OF INDIA 

New Delhi, the 1st February 1972 
S . O . 582. - In pursuance of clause (b ) of clause ( d ) 
of sub - section ( 2 ) of section 116 ( C ) of the Representa 
tion of the People Act, 1951, the Election Commission 
hereby publishes the Judgement of the Supreme Court 
of India , dated the 3rd January, 1972 in Civil Appeal 
No 1178 - (NCE ) of 1971 against the Order dated the 
22nd July , 1971 of the High Court of Madhya Pradesh , 
Jabalpur, in Election Petition No 6 of 1971. 

IN THE SUPREME COURT OF INDIA 
CIVIL APPELLATE JURISDICTION 

CIVIL APPEAL No. 1178 OF" 1971 
Sri Chandrakant Shukla - - Appellant 

Vs. 
Maharaja Martand Singh - Respondent 

JUDGMENT 
HEGDE, J. — This appeal under section 116A of the 
Representation of People Act, 1951, is directed against 
the decision of the Madhya Pradesh High Court Dis 
missing the election petition Aled by tho appellant on 
the ground that the same ls barred by limitation . 

The election petitioner is a voter in the Rewa Parlla 
mentary constituency . The olection with which we are 
concerned in this appeal was to the Parliament. That 
election was held on March 1, 1971. The counting was 
over on March 10 , 1971. The same day the election 
results were announced . The respondent was declared 
elected . The election results were published in the 
official gazette on March 15 , 1971. The election petition 
was Aled on April 29, 1971. 

Section 81 ( 1) of the Representation of People Act, 
1951 , provides that an election petition calling in ques 
tion any election may be presented on one or more of 
the grounds specified in sub - section ( 1 ) of section 100 
and section 101 to the High Court by any candidate at 
such election or by any elector within forty - five days 
from , but not earllar than , the date of election of the 
returned candidate , or if there are more than one 
returned candidate at the election and the dates of their 
election are different, the later of those two dates 
Section 67A Says 
" For the purposes of this Act, the date on which a 

candidate is declared by returning officer under 
the provisions of section 53 or section 66 , to be 
elected to a House of Parliament or of the 
Legislature of a State shall be the date of elec 

tion of that candidate. " 
Reading these two sections together it is clear that the 
election petition should have been Aled within forty 
five days from March 10, 1971 . Hence, the petition filed 
by the appellant is clearly barred by limitation .

भारत निर्वाचन आयोग 


प्रादेश 


TE GETT, 31 TFTCT, 1972 


Through it was contended before the High Court that 
the election petition wag Aled within time, that conten 
tion was not pressed before us. The only contention 
taken before us was that there was sufficient cause to 
condone the delay in Aling the election petition , As we 
are unable to accept that contention we are not examin 
ing the question whether the High Court had any 
power to condone the delay in Aling the election peti 
tion . 

Now coming to the question whether there was suffi 
cient cause for condoning the delay the High Court he d 
that no such sufficient cause was shown. This is essen 
tially & Anding of fact and this court ordinarily does 
not Interfere with the decision of the High Court on 
questions of fact. Further , the relief asked for is essen . 
tially a discretionary relief and when a trial court exer 
Çises its discretion an appellate court is reluctant to 
interfere with that discretion unless there are very good 
grounds for doing so . 

Now , turning to the facts of this case , in the applica 
tion Aled for condonation of delay only reason adyanced 
is that the pititioner believes that he has Aled his elec 
tion petition within time but if the court takes a 
different view then the delay may be condoned . No 
cause was shown for condoning the delay. But while 
glving evidence in the court the petitioner went much 
further . He deposed that before Aling the election 
petition be consulted Shri Hari Shankar Saxena, 
an advocate , and that advocate told him that 
he has to file the election petitin within forty 
five days of the publication of the result of the 
election in the gazette. No such vehement was 
made in the petition wherein he sought the con 
donation of the delay nor Shri Hari Shankar Saxena 
was examined as witness in the case. Under those cir 
cumstances the High Court rightly disbelived the evid 
ence of the petitioner to the effect that he had consulted 
Shri Hari Shankar Saxena before Aling the election 
petition . The High Court was of the opinion that the 
petitioner s version that he consulted Shri Hari Shankar 
Saxena is an after - thought. We agree with that con 
clusion . The petitioner has given no satisfactory ex 
planation for the delay in Aling the election petition . 
Hence his election petition was rightly dismissed by the 
High Court . 

In the result this appeal fails and the same is dis 
missed with costs . 

- - - - J. 
K . S . Hegde,

Rules made thereunder ;

by section 11 of the said Act, the Election Commission 
for the reasons recorded on the representation made by 


MINISTRY OF SHIPPING AND TRANSPORT 
( Directorate General of Shipping ) 

ORDER 
Bombay , the 3rd February 1972 
S .O . 584. — In pursuance of note ( 3 ) of the Schedule 
to the Notification of the Government of India in the 
Ministry of Shipping & Transport relating to scales of 
provision for seamen , No. s . O , 2169 , dated 21st June, 
1967, and in furtherance of the order of Director Gene 
ral of Shipping No . 9 (21) - CRA / 67, dated 3rd February , 
1972, I, R . Doraiswamy. Director General of Shippine, 
being satisfied that rice supply position in the country 
has improved , hereby order that with effect from the 
date of this order the total dally scale of cercal rations 
of 670 grams shall be restored to 400 grams rice and 
170 grams wheat, if procurement is made in India . 

[No. 9 (21)- CRA / 07.) 

R . DORAISWAMY, 
Director General of Shipping .
